How To Read This Report

This report presents simulator quality evidence across multiple dimensions, each collapsible for easy navigation:

  • Findings Dashboard (above): High-level signal showing must-fix errors, known gaps, and coverage gaps with direct links.
  • GFS Sizing Test Status: Standalone validation results with case-level boundary and integration checks across modifier boundaries, weekly/monthly/yearly ranges, and engine integration.
  • Mutation Testing Status: Shows which logic mutations are caught by scenarios; blind spots indicate missing test coverage.
  • Golden Snapshot Registry: Baseline state checkpoints (day 365 & 730) for long-running scenarios; mismatches appear in the dashboard.
  • Contract Rule Coverage: Lists all contract rules with which scenarios cover each; uncovered rules appear in the dashboard.
  • Scenario Validation (organized by layer 1–4): Scenario-by-scenario validation with expected vs. actual lifecycle, plus timeline samples. Use the toolbar to expand/collapse all, search by scenario ID or rule, and filter by status or layer. For each scenario, read left-to-right: Expected Lifecycle defines what should happen, Actual Lifecycle shows engine behavior, Lifecycle Timeline Samples provides day-index checkpoints.

All sections expand on demand. Known engine gaps are explicitly labeled and excluded from failures.

Workflow Scope

Primary validation workflow (active gates):

  • npm test — deterministic scenario validation and core invariants.
  • npm run compare:veeam — oracle comparison against captured Veeam Calculator Details values, including optional file-type size matching when baseline values are present.
  • npm run verify:known-veeam-deltas — validates only approved calculator deltas remain.
  • npm run test:quality — consolidated quality pipeline (GFS sizing, mutation testing, lifecycle contracts, and golden snapshots).

Archived/exploratory tests (kept for research, not required CI gates):

  • archive:test:idealized-gfs — idealized clone-theory exploration.
  • archive:test:live-weekly-gfs — historical weekly live-capture hypothesis exploration.
  • archive:test:live-period-gfs — historical monthly/yearly live-capture hypothesis exploration.

Timeout note: run these gates as separate commands to keep each execution bounded and resilient. If any approved behavior fix is applied, restart the workflow from scenario 1 and rerun all active gates.
